Even when I’m calm, I still feel like they’re keeping notes on me or testing me with little comments. My thoughts move too fast. I got really worked up during a staff meeting and started acting out this whole fall-prevention drill, moving chairs around and waving my arms like I was showing everybody what to do. I couldn’t really stop myself, even when people were telling me to sit down.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe that your supervisor and coworkers are keeping notes on you or testing you, or is that just a suspicion that crosses your mind sometimes?\n\nPatient: I mean… I really do believe it. It’s not just a quick thought that comes and goes. I just worry a lot about whether I can even go back to caregiving work safely.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the beliefs you have about your supervisor and coworkers are definitely true, or can you consider the possibility that they might not be?\n\nPatient: Honestly… I feel pretty sure it’s true. Even when I’m calm and I try to think it through, I still feel like my supervisor and a couple people at work are keeping notes on me. I got really worked up during a staff meeting and started acting out this whole fall-prevention drill, moving chairs around and waving my arms like I was showing everybody what to do. I couldn’t really stop myself, even when people were telling me to sit down.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe that your supervisor and coworkers are keeping notes on you or testing you, or is that just a suspicion that crosses your mind sometimes?\n\nPatient: I mean… I really do believe it. It’s not just a quick thought that comes and goes. Even when I’m calm, I still feel like my supervisor and a couple people there are keeping notes on me, watching how I act, and making little comments to test me. But when I go check, there’s nobody there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the belief that your supervisor and coworkers are keeping notes on you is something that might not be true?\n\nPatient: I guess I can say maybe it’s possible, but honestly, even when I’m calm, I still feel pretty sure they’re doing it. My supervisor and a couple coworkers… it feels like they’re watching me, keeping notes, making little comments to test me, like they’re trying to show I’m not safe to be around the seniors.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble sticking to one topic when talking to others?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. I just worry a lot about whether I can even go back to caregiving work safely.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the beliefs you have about your supervisor and coworkers are definitely true, or can you consider the possibility that they might not be?\n\nPatient: Honestly… I feel pretty sure it’s true.
